🌺 Kihei · Maui

Maui Beach Vacation Club

⭐ Condo-style Kihei resort with pool, hot tub, BBQ
$236/night$3,306 total · 14 nights · 2 bed/2 bath, sleeps 6
Sat Dec 19 → Sat Jan 2
Open listing
Cheaper than 99% of 2BR weeks in Maui
✈️ Flights not priced yet
🛏️ Villa & room configuration2 Bed / 2 Bath · Sleeps 6 · 939 sq ft

Two-bedroom units: king bed in master, two twin beds in guest bedroom, queen sofa sleeper in living area, two bathrooms; sleeps up to six. Full kitchen with refrigerator, stove, microwave, coffee maker. In-unit washer/dryer and private balcony; view varies, with ocean views in select units. Resort gym: not confirmed in sources.

🏓 Kids’ on-site action3/10 Limited

Confirmed family features are an outdoor pool, hot tub, BBQ area, activity desk, and restaurant; children 12 and under must be supervised and there is no lifeguard.

☀️ December pool & sunRisky

Sources describe a single outdoor fish-shaped pool and a hot tub; the pool is not heated, while hot tub heating is not confirmed. Late-December high, shade, and wind are not confirmed.

🌴 Surrounding family fun8/10

Steps from Mai Poina/Maalaea Bay beaches and a 5-minute walk to the whale sanctuary HQ; Wailea Beach about 7.4 mi and airport about 9.3 mi/15 min. On-site sushi restaurant; snorkeling/scuba/kayaking nearby; Maui Ocean Center under 15 min.

Still being researched: gym.

🛏️ Villa & room configuration2 Bed / 2 Bath · 2 Queen + Sofabed

Two-Bedroom Suite is 1335 sqft with 1 sofabed and 2 queen beds. Full kitchen with cookware and dishwasher, private washer/dryer, and private outdoor lanai. Balcony listed.

🏓 Kids’ on-site action5/10 Fair

No water slides, lazy river, splash area, game room, kids club, mini golf, or playground are mentioned. On-site tennis and basketball courts plus daily crafts, games, and poolside barbecues provide activity. Family-friendly resort.

🏋️ Gym2/10 Affiliate gym only

Kahana Villa does not have a fitness center; guests can use the one at affiliate Kaanapali Shores about 1.5 miles southeast for $10/day or $28/week. Some booking sites list a fitness center or health club on-site, but the resort FAQ says otherwise.

☀️ December pool & sunRisky

Sources describe one outdoor pool and a hot tub, with a reviewer reporting the pool was not heated. No source gives late-December highs or shade/wind specifics, so sunny pool time is less certain than at Hawaii timeshares with heated pools.

🌴 Surrounding family fun9/10

Pohaku Beach is 2 minutes' walk; Kahana Beach 12 minutes' walk for snorkeling. The Beach House 2 minutes' walk, Miso Phat 800 ft, Maui Brewing Co 7 minutes' walk. Lahaina 16 min/6.3 mi; Kapalua Beach 2.3 mi; Napili Bay 2.0 mi.

It has a full kitchen, in-unit washer/dryer, and a patio or balcony with variable view. A separate source lists a two-bedroom suite at 1005 sq ft with 1 king, 2 single beds, and 1 sofa bed.

🏓 Kids’ on-site action7/10 Good

The 3.5-acre Superpool complex has cascading waterfalls, hidden grottos, winding waterways, a family-friendly Shipwreck Beach and waterslides, plus a swim-up pool bar. ☀️ December pool & sunGreat · ~82°F

The two-bedroom villa sleeps six adults comfortably: a primary bedroom with king-size bed, walk-through closet and lānaʻi access, plus a second bedroom with king-size bed and private bathroom. The living room has an HDTV, sofa bed and armoire, with a kitchenette (two-burner cooktop, microwave, dishwasher, refrigerator), dining banquette and a stacked washer-and-dryer. The villas sit across the street from the Sheraton Kauai Resort and all amenities are shared, so the gym is shared with the sister resort. Recent reviewer complaints about broken or crowded equipment: not confirmed.

Specific shade, wind exposure and typical late-December daytime highs are not confirmed in these sources.

🌴 Surrounding family fun8/10

Poipu Beach and Lawai Beach are about a 5-minute drive, Kiahuna Beach is an 8-minute walk, and Poipu Shopping Village is a 7-minute walk with restaurants such as Keoki's Paradise and Pietro's Pizza nearby. Spouting Horn is about 2 miles, Shipwreck's Beach 1.7 miles, and Old Koloa Town roughly 10 minutes by car.

🏋️ Gym5/10 Fitness center, classes

A fitness center is listed among resort amenities, and daily fitness classes are included in the daily resort charge. Whether it is shared with a sister resort is not confirmed. No recent reviewer complaints about broken or crowded equipment were found in the sources.

☀️ December pool & sunGood

Sources confirm outdoor pools and hot tubs, but counts conflict: 4 outdoor pools and a hot tub vs 2 outdoor pools and 2 hot tubs. Heating, shade/wind, and typical late-December high are not confirmed.

🌴 Surrounding family fun7/10

Kings Shops are 2.2 mi away; Hapuna Beach State Recreation Area is 7.3 mi; Puako Petroglyph Park is a 10-minute drive. Roy's Waikoloa Bar & Grill is under 10 minutes and Sansei is an 8-minute drive. A free shuttle serves Waikoloa Beach Resort and Hilton Waikoloa Village for a saltwater lagoon and spa.

Three pools including Redwood Pool with a slide, plus Pinocchio's Workshop kids club, Grizzly Game Arcade, playground, and activities like scavenger hunts and crafts. No lazy river or mini golf confirmed; tennis is listed in one source but another says no tennis.

🏋️ Gym7/10 Full resort gym

Eureka Fitness Center is described as a complete exercise center, and fitness classes are listed. It is shared with Disney's Grand Californian Hotel & Spa. No recent reviewer complaints found in sources.

☀️ December pool & sunGood · heated

Pools are heated to 82°F/28°C and open daily in winter; the resort has three pools (Fountain, Redwood, Mariposa) and three hot tubs, with a hot tub near each pool. Typical late-December highs and shade/wind are not confirmed by these sources.

🌴 Surrounding family fun9/10

Theme parks and Downtown Disney are walkable: Disneyland Park about 4-10 minutes on foot, with a private entrance to Disney California Adventure. The beach is a short drive; Knott's Berry Farm and LEGOLAND are nearby but drive times, zoo, and hiking are not confirmed.

Sledding, tubing and ice skating specifics are not confirmed.

Typical late-December snow conditions and temperatures, and sledding or snow play for kids, are not confirmed in these sources.

🌴 Surrounding family fun9/10

Ski-in/ski-out at Canyons Village, with restaurants, shopping, and activities immediately out the door; Red Pine Gondola and Orange Bubble are steps away.
